2026-10-22T05:19:35.7012106Z [36;1muv run --no-project python scripts/check-kernel-experiment-tags.py[0m

2026-10-22T05:19:35.7178985Z env:
2026-10-22T05:19:35.7179296Z   UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/uv-python-dir
2026-10-22T05:19:35.7179749Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-22T05:19:35.7185875Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Rerun the Decode B1/B4/B5/B6 serving after allowing one-FP8-code RoPE differences in the numeric gate

Why: run 39714885018 (993077c) stopped in the MegaMoE gate allocation
before model startup. check-firmus-megamoe.py runs
check-firmus-decode-kernels.py in-process. Its check_rope_fp8_kv demanded
bitwise equality of the FP8 Q output against the unfused reference, and B6's
new Decode QK-norm/RoPE kernel (head-parallel, four heads per warp) failed
on one case: 126 rows, int64 positions, contiguous Q. relative_l2 was
6.5e-5 and max_scaled_error 0.011, i.e. single FP8 codes flipped by a
different FP32 reduction order in the RMSNorm sum. The other sizes in that
check (1, 3, 31 and 126 with int32) were bitwise equal, which fits
rounding-order noise rather than a layout bug. The dense small-M checks
(both host layouts) passed before it.

What:
- scripts/check-firmus-decode-kernels.py, check_rope_fp8_kv compare():
  V (a pure cast), untouched cache rows and the padding sink must still be
  bitwise equal. Q and K, which go through RMSNorm and RoPE, must now
  satisfy the existing check_numerics bounds and differ by at most one FP8
  code. Bit identity is not required by the precision rules; FP order
  differences are allowed and the tolerance is not otherwise widened.
- sglang.sbatch no longer runs check-firmus-decode-kernels.py as a
  separate step. The MegaMoE gate already runs it, so 993077c ran it twice.
Configuration, overlays and image (39711569005) are as in 993077c.
